/* E1-25 · Sticky feature swap: text steps scroll, one sticky frame changes its image per step. */
.ss {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1.1fr);
  gap: clamp(24px, 5vw, 96px);
  padding-inline: var(--pad, 48px);
  color: #1D1B18;
}
.ss__steps { grid-column: 1; grid-row: 1; }
.ss__media {
  grid-column: 2;
  grid-row: 1;
  position: sticky;
  top: 10vh;
  block-size: 80vh;
  border-radius: 20px;
  overflow: hidden;
  background: #E6E1D8;
}
.ss__img { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: cover; clip-
path: inset(100% 0 0 0); }
.ss__img:first-child { clip-path: inset(0); }
.ss__dots { position: absolute; inset-inline-start: 16px; bottom: 16px; display: flex; gap: 6px; margin: 
0; padding: 0; list-style: none; }
.ss__dots li { inline-size: 26px; block-size: 3px; border-radius: 3px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 
0.45); transition: background-color 0.4s; }
.ss__dots li.is-on { background: #fff; }
.ss__step { min-block-size: 80vh; display: flex; flex-direction: column; justify-content: center; padding-
block: 10vh; }
.ss__step h3 { font: 500 clamp(30px, 3.4vw, 54px)/1.1 var(--font-serif, serif); }
.ss__step p { margin-top: 14px; max-inline-size: 34ch; font-size: 18px; color: #5E5850; }
.ss__step { opacity: 0.28; transition: opacity 0.5s; }
.ss__step.is-on { opacity: 1; }
.ss__inline { display: none; }
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .ss { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }

element.js
// E1-25 · Sticky feature swap — each text step activates its image with a wipe; dots show progress.
import { gsap, ScrollTrigger, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { wipe: 0.9, ...opts };
  const steps = [...root.querySelectorAll('.ss__step')];
  const imgs = [...root.querySelectorAll('.ss__img')];
  const dots = [...root.querySelectorAll('.ss__dots li' )];
  let current = -1;
  let z = 1;
  const show = (i, animate) => {
    if (i === current || !imgs[i]) return;
    const prev = current;
    current = i;
    steps.forEach((s, k) => s.classList.toggle('is-on', k === i));
    dots.forEach((d, k) => d.classList.toggle('is-on', k === i));
    const img = imgs[i];
    img.style.zIndex = ++z;
    const down = prev < i; // scrolling down wipes up from the bottom, scrolling up wipes down from the  
top
    if (!animate) { gsap.set(img, { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)'  }); return; }
    gsap.fromTo(img, { clipPath: down ? 'inset(100% 0% 0% 0%)'  : 'inset(0% 0% 100% 0%)' , scale: 1.08 },
      { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)' , scale: 1, duration: o.wipe, ease: 'power3.inOut', overwrite: true 
});
  };
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add({ motion: MQ.motion, desktop: '(min-width: 768px)'  }, ({ conditions }) => {
    if (!conditions.desktop) return;
    show(0, false);
    const triggers = steps.map((step, i) => ScrollTrigger.create({
      trigger: step,
      start: 'top 55%' ,
      end: 'bottom 55%' ,
      onToggle: (self) => self.isActive && show(i, conditions.motion),
    }));
    return () => { triggers.forEach((t) => t.kill()); current = -1; };
  });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

element.css
/* E1-26 · Map route journey: a pinned map where the route draws itself and a marker rides it with scroll.  
*/
.mr { --ink: #20312B; --accent: #D9572B; color: var(--ink); }
.mr__pin {
  block-size: 100svh;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 0.9fr) minmax(0, 1.1fr);
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4vw;
  padding-inline: var(--pad, 48px);
}
.mr__title { font: 500 clamp(34px, 4vw, 64px)/1.05 var(--font-serif, serif); }
.mr__meta { margin-top: 10px; color: #6B776F; }
.mr__stops { margin: 28px 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; display: grid; gap: 14px; counter-reset: s; }
.mr__stops li { position: relative; padding-inline-start: 34px; font-size: 17px; color: #9AA59F; 
transition: color 0.4s; }
.mr__stops li::before {
  counter-increment: s;
  content: counter(s);
  position: absolute;
  inset-inline-start: 0;
  top: 0.1em;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  inline-size: 22px;
  block-size: 22px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  border: 1.5px solid currentColor;
  font-size: 12px;
}
.mr__stops li.is-on { color: var(--ink); }
.mr__stops li.is-on::before { background: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); color: #fff; }
.mr__map { inline-size: 100%; max-block-size: 86vh; }
.mr__land { fill: #E4DCC9; }

round; }
.mr__route { fill: none; stroke: var(--accent); stroke-width: 5; stroke-linecap: round; }
.mr__dots circle { fill: #fff; stroke: #B9AE98; stroke-width: 3; transition: fill 0.3s, stroke 0.3s; }

@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .mr__pin { grid-template-columns: 1fr; align-content: center; gap: 8px; }
  .mr__map { max-block-size: 52vh; }
  .mr__stops { margin-top: 12px; gap: 6px; }
  .mr__stops li { font-size: 14px; }

element.js
// E1-26 · Map route journey — pinned: the route draws, a rider follows it (MotionPath), stops light up in  
order.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { DrawSVGPlugin } from 'gsap/DrawSVGPlugin';
import { MotionPathPlugin } from 'gsap/MotionPathPlugin';
gsap.registerPlugin(DrawSVGPlugin, MotionPathPlugin);
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { distance: '+=220%', ...opts };
  const pin = root.querySelector('.mr__pin');
  const route = root.querySelector('.mr__route');
  const rider = root.querySelector('.mr__rider');
  const items = [...root.querySelectorAll('.mr__stops li' )];
  const dots = [...root.querySelectorAll('.mr__dots circle' )];
  const marks = items.map((li) => Number(li.dataset.at) || 0);
  const light = (p) => marks.forEach((m, i) => {
    const on = p >= m - 0.005;
    items[i].classList.toggle('is-on', on);
    dots[i]?.classList.toggle('is-on', on);
  });
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    const tl = gsap.timeline({
      defaults: { ease: 'none' },
      scrollTrigger: { trigger: pin, start: 'top top' , end: o.distance, pin: true, scrub: 0.8, onUpdate: 
(self) => light(self.progress) },
    });
    tl.fromTo(route, { drawSVG: '0%' }, { drawSVG: '100%' }, 0)
      .to(rider, { motionPath: { path: '#mr-route', align: '#mr-route', alignOrigin: [0.5, 0.5] } }, 0);
    light(0);
    return () => gsap.set([route, rider], { clearProps: 'all' });
  });
  mm.add(MQ.reduce, () => {
    // Static: the whole route, every stop lit, the rider parked at the finish.
    light(1);
    gsap.set(rider, { motionPath: { path: '#mr-route', align: '#mr-route', alignOrigin: [0.5, 0.5], start: 
1, end: 1 } });
  });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

E1-27עקומה  על  שטס  מוצר

עריכתי,  ואנרגטי  נועז,  שובב:  אופי· מלא:  מובייל· קלה  עלות· וסיפור  גלילה· 1 טייר
P21 P27 P35 P07 :מנופים· N03 N02 N06 N11 N10 N07 N25 :נישות
ונקייה  גבוהה(,  PNG/WebP) שקוף  רקע  על  אחת  מוצר  תמונת:  נכסים· gsap 3.15 (ScrollTrigger, MotionPathPlugin) :ספריות

element.css
/* E1-27 · Curve flight: one product travels along a curve through several sections as you scroll. */
.cf { position: relative; color: #142235; }
.cf__svg { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; pointer-events: none; }
.cf__path { fill: none; stroke: rgba(20, 34, 53, 0.12); stroke-width: 2; stroke-dasharray: 4 10; vector-
effect: non-scaling-stroke; }
.cf__item {
  position: absolute;
  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  z-index: 2;
  inline-size: clamp(110px, 12vw, 190px);
  block-size: auto;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 30px 30px rgba(20, 40, 70, 0.25));
  pointer-events: none;
}
.cf__sec { position: relative; z-index: 1; min-block-size: 100svh; display: grid; align-content: center; 
padding-inline: var(--pad, 48px); }
.cf__sec h2 { font: 700 clamp(42px, 6vw, 96px)/1 var(--font-sans, system-ui); letter-spacing: -0.03em; }
.cf__sec p { margin-top: 14px; max-inline-size: 30ch; font-size: 19px; color: #4A5A70; }
.cf__sec--a, .cf__sec--c { justify-items: start; }
.cf__sec--b { justify-items: end; text-align: end; }
.cf__sec--c { justify-items: center; text-align: center; align-content: end; padding-bottom: 12vh; }
.cf__cta { display: inline-block; margin-top: 22px; padding: 14px 28px; border-radius: 999px; background: 
#142235; color: #fff; font-weight: 600; text-decoration: none; }
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .cf__sec { align-content: end; padding-bottom: 16vh; }
  .cf__sec--b { justify-items: start; text-align: start; }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .cf__item, .cf__svg { display: none; }
}
element.js
// E1-27 · Curve flight — the item follows an SVG path stretched over several sections (MotionPath +  
scrub).
import { gsap, MQ, isRTL } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { MotionPathPlugin } from 'gsap/MotionPathPlugin';
gsap.registerPlugin(MotionPathPlugin);
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { tilt: 18, ...opts };
  const item = root.querySelector('.cf__item');
  const path = root.querySelector('.cf__path');


## Page 106

עקומה  על  שטס  מוצר· E1-27תלת־ממד  בלי:  1 טייר —  10 פרק
106
  // The path is drawn for RTL pages (text on the right first). On LTR pages mirror it: x →  1000 − x.
  // Format: absolute commands with "x y" pairs separated by commas, in a 0..1000 wide viewBox.
  const original = path.getAttribute('d');
  if (!isRTL(root)) path.setAttribute('d', original.replace(/(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?) (-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/g , (m, x, 
y) => `${1000 - x} ${y}`));
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    const tl = gsap.timeline({
      defaults: { ease: 'none' },
      scrollTrigger: { trigger: root, start: 'top top' , end: 'bottom bottom' , scrub: 1, 
invalidateOnRefresh: true },
    });
    tl.to(item, {
      motionPath: { path, align: path, alignOrigin: [0.5, 0.5], autoRotate: false },
      duration: 1,
    }, 0)
      // A little life on the way: lean into the curves, shrink in the middle, land upright and big.
      .to(item, { rotation: o.tilt, duration: 0.25, ease: 'sine.inOut' }, 0)
      .to(item, { rotation: -o.tilt, scale: 0.8, duration: 0.35, ease: 'sine.inOut' }, 0.25)
      .to(item, { rotation: 0, scale: 1.15, duration: 0.4, ease: 'sine.inOut' }, 0.6);
    return () => gsap.set(item, { clearProps: 'all' });
  });
  return () => { mm.revert(); path.setAttribute('d', original); };
}

element.css
/* E1-28 · Device screen scroll: a laptop (phone on mobile) whose screen scrolls a full-page screenshot.  
*/
.dv { color: #12141A; }
.dv__pin { block-size: 100svh; display: grid; align-content: center; justify-items: center; gap: 4vh; 
padding-inline: var(--pad, 48px); perspective: 1600px; overflow: hidden; }
.dv__text { text-align: center; }
.dv__title { font: 700 clamp(32px, 4.4vw, 70px)/1.05 var(--font-sans, system-ui); letter-spacing: -0.02em; 
}
.dv__lead { margin-top: 10px; color: #5C6270; font-size: 18px; }
.dv__device { inline-size: min(980px, 82vw); transform-origin: 50% 100%; }
.dv__screen {
  position: relative;
  aspect-ratio: 16 / 10;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: clamp(8px, 1.1vw, 14px) solid #15171C;
  border-radius: clamp(12px, 1.6vw, 22px) clamp(12px, 1.6vw, 22px) 6px 6px;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 40px 80px -30px rgba(18, 20, 26, 0.45);
}
.dv__shot { position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: auto; max-inline-size: 
none; }
.dv__base {
  block-size: clamp(12px, 1.6vw, 22px);
  margin-inline: -6%;
  border-radius: 0 0 18px 18px;
  background: linear-gradient(#D7DAE1, #A9AEB9);
  clip-path: polygon(0 0, 100% 0, 97% 100%, 3% 100%);
}
@media (max-width: 767px) {
  .dv__device { inline-size: min(300px, 70vw); }
  .dv__screen { aspect-ratio: 9 / 19; border-width: 10px; border-radius: 34px; }
  .dv__base { display: none; }
  .dv__shot { inline-size: 250%; } /* show the desktop screenshot at phone scale: crop the start side */
  .dv__shot:dir(rtl) { left: auto; right: 0; }
}
element.js
// E1-28 · Device screen scroll — pinned: the device tilts up to face you, then its screenshot scrolls  
inside.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root, opts = {}) {
  const o = { distance: '+=200%', tilt: 28, ...opts };


## Page 109

שנגלל  מכשיר  מסך· E1-28תלת־ממד  בלי:  1 טייר —  10 פרק
109
  const pin = root.querySelector('.dv__pin');
  const device = root.querySelector('.dv__device');
  const screen = root.querySelector('.dv__screen');
  const shot = root.querySelector('.dv__shot');
  const travel = () => -Math.max(0, shot.offsetHeight - screen.clientHeight);
  const mm = gsap.matchMedia();
  mm.add(MQ.motion, () => {
    const tl = gsap.timeline({
      defaults: { ease: 'none' },
      scrollTrigger: { trigger: pin, start: 'top top' , end: o.distance, pin: true, scrub: 0.8, 
invalidateOnRefresh: true },
    });
    tl.fromTo(device, { rotationX: o.tilt, scale: 0.86, y: 40 }, { rotationX: 0, scale: 1, y: 0, duration: 
0.25, ease: 'power2.out' })
      .to(shot, { y: travel, duration: 0.75 });
    return () => gsap.set([device, shot], { clearProps: 'all' });
  });
  return () => mm.revert();
}

element.css
/* E2-01 · Product choreography: one sticky full-screen canvas above the sections draws a 3D product that  
flies
   between "anchors" (empty boxes in the layout). The posters inside the anchors are the still version. */
.pc { position: relative; isolation: isolate; }
.pc__stage {
  position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 2; pointer-events: none;
  block-size: 100vh; block-size: 100lvh;              /* lvh: the canvas still covers the screen when the  
URL bar hides */
  margin-block-end: -100vh; margin-block-end: -100lvh; /* takes no room: the sections slide underneath */
}
.pc__canvas { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.6s; }
.pc[data-gl='live'] .pc__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.pc__sec { position: relative; z-index: 1; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; align-items: 
center; gap: clamp(24px, 4vw, 72px); min-block-size: 100svh; padding: 12vh var(--pad, 48px); overflow: 
clip; }
.pc__anchor { position: relative; justify-self: center; inline-size: min(28vw, 340px); block-size: 
min(70svh, 640px); }
.pc__poster { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: contain; rotate: var(--rz, 
0deg); transition: opacity 0.5s; }
.pc[data-gl='live'] .pc__poster { opacity: 0; }
/* floor shadow — JS sets --dock (0..1): how much the product is standing in this anchor right now */
.pc__anchor::after {
  content: ''; position: absolute; inset-inline: 16%; inset-block-end: 0; block-size: 7%; border-radius: 
50%; pointer-events: none;
  background: radial-gradient(closest-side, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4), transparent);
  opacity: var(--dock, 1);
}
/* hero: the product stands on a giant faded wordmark */
.pc__sec--hero { grid-template-columns: 1fr; grid-template-rows: 1fr auto; padding-block: 10vh 8vh; 
background: #13281E; color: #EDE9DF; }
.pc__mark {
  position: absolute; inset: 0; z-index: -1; display: grid; place-items: center; margin: 0; user-select: 
none;
  font: 700 min(42vw, 78svh)/0.8 var(--font-condensed);
  background: linear-gradient(#2B5A41, #13281E 82%); -webkit-background-clip: text; background-clip: text; 
color: transparent;
}
.pc__sec--hero .pc__anchor { block-size: min(60svh, 600px); }
.pc__bar { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: end; justify-content: space-between; gap: 20px 
40px; }
.pc__title { max-inline-size: 15ch; margin: 0; font: 800 clamp(30px, 3.6vw, 56px)/1.04 var(--font-sans); 
letter-spacing: -0.02em; text-wrap: balance; }
.pc__cta { display: inline-block; padding: 15px 30px; border-radius: 999px; background: #E3B23C; color: 
#13281E; font-weight: 800; text-decoration: none; }
.pc__cta:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid currentColor; outline-offset: 3px; }
.pc__copy { max-inline-size: 30rem; justify-self: center; }
.pc__copy h2 { margin: 0; font: 800 clamp(34px, 4.4vw, 68px)/1.02 var(--font-sans); letter-spacing: 
-0.025em; text-wrap: balance; }
.pc__copy p { margin: 18px 0 0; font-size: clamp(17px, 1.35vw, 20px); }
.pc__specs { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 14px 34px; margin: 32px 0 0; }
.pc__specs div { display: grid; gap: 2px; }
.pc__specs dt { font-size: 15px; color: #4A5E50; }
.pc__specs dd { margin: 0; font: 800 clamp(22px, 2vw, 30px)/1.15 var(--font-sans); }
.pc__sec--wall { background: #D3DCCB; color: #13281E; }
.pc__sec--wall p { color: #31453A; }
.pc__sec--lid { background: #1F1E1B; color: #EDE9DF; }
.pc__sec--lid p { color: #C9C4B8; }
.pc__sec--buy { background: #D9D4C7; color: #13281E; }
.pc__sec--buy .pc__cta { margin-top: 22px; background: #13281E; color: #EDE9DF; }
.pc__price { font: 800 clamp(40px, 4vw, 64px)/1 var(--font-sans); }

element.js
// E2-01 · Product choreography (scroll anchors) — one real 3D product (a GLB) travels down the page.
// The layout holds "anchors": empty boxes placed like images. Each one says where the product stands, how  
big it is
// (the box height) and at what angle (data-rot="x,y,z" in degrees; data-spin = whole turns on the way  
in).
// Every frame the anchors' rects are read: while an anchor is near the middle of the screen the product  
is docked in
// it (and scrolls with it); in between it flies to the next anchor — eased, with a small swoop toward the  
camera.
// One sticky full-screen canvas above the sections (pointer-events: none) draws it. A poster inside each  
anchor is
// the no-WebGL / reduced-motion version: the same layout as still images. Pointer and scroll speed tilt  
it a little.
import { gsap, MQ, scrollVelocity, clamp, lerp } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { THREE, island, createRenderer, studioEnvironment, sizeToElement, loopWhenVisible, loadGLB, 
normalizeModel, worldPerPixel, disposeRenderer } from '@/motion-core/three-kit.js';
const D2R = Math.PI / 180;
const smooth = (a, b, x) => { const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a)); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
export function init(root, { model = '/assets/models/water-bottle.glb', hold = 0.22, fill = 0.9, intro = 
true } = {}) {
  const stage = root.querySelector('.pc__stage');
  const anchors = [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-pc-anchor]')];
  const isl = island(root, { minTier: 2 });
  if (!isl.ok || !anchors.length || matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches) { isl.fail(); return isl.dispose; }
  let renderer;
  try {
    renderer = createRenderer(isl.canvas, { alpha: true, tier: isl.tier });
  } catch (err) { isl.fail(err); return isl.dispose; }
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  studioEnvironment(renderer, scene, 0.9);
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.8);
  key.position.set(3, 5, 6);
  const rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.4);
  rim.position.set(-5, 2, -4);
  scene.add(key, rim);
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(20, 1, 0.1, 100); // narrow lens: little distortion away from  
centre
  camera.position.set(0, 0, 14);
  let W = 1, H = 1, wpp = 0.01;
  const stopSize = sizeToElement(renderer, camera, stage, (w, h) => { W = w; H = h; wpp = 
worldPerPixel(camera, h); });
  const poses = anchors.map((a) => {
    const [x = 0, y = 0, z = 0] = (a.dataset.rot || '0,0,0').split(',').map(Number);
    return { q: new THREE.Quaternion().setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(x * D2R, y * D2R, z * D2R)), spin: 
Number(a.dataset.spin || 0) };
  });
  const product = new THREE.Group();

  scene.add(product);
  const q = new THREE.Quaternion(), qSpin = new THREE.Quaternion(), qTilt = new THREE.Quaternion();
  const Y = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), tiltE = new THREE.Euler();
  const drop = { p: intro ? 0 : 1 };
  const ptr = { x: 0, y: 0, tx: 0, ty: 0 };
  const dock = anchors.map(() => -1);
  let lean = 0, disposed = false, stopLoop = () => {}, introTween = null;
  const onMove = (e) => { ptr.tx = e.clientX / innerWidth - 0.5; ptr.ty = e.clientY / innerHeight - 0.5; 
};
  if (matchMedia(MQ.fine).matches) addEventListener('pointermove', onMove, { passive: true });
  const frame = (dt, t) => {
    const sr = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    const pts = anchors.map((a) => {
      const r = a.getBoundingClientRect();
      return { x: r.left - sr.left + r.width / 2, y: r.top - sr.top + r.height / 2, h: r.height };
    });
    // which two anchors are we between? i = the one above the middle line, e = eased progress to the next
    const focus = H / 2, last = pts.length - 1;
    let i = 0, e = 0;
    if (pts[last].y <= focus) i = last;
    else if (pts[0].y < focus) {
      while (i < last - 1 && pts[i + 1].y <= focus) i++;
      e = smooth(hold, 1 - hold, (focus - pts[i].y) / (pts[i + 1].y - pts[i].y));
    }
    const j = Math.min(i + 1, last), a = pts[i], b = pts[j];
    const swoop = Math.sin(Math.PI * e), fall = 1 - drop.p;
    const x = lerp(a.x, b.x, e);
    const y = lerp(a.y, b.y, e) + Math.sin(t * 1.3) * 5 - fall * H * 0.9;
    const h = lerp(a.h, b.h, e) * fill * (1 + swoop * 0.12);
    product.position.set((x - W / 2) * wpp, -(y - H / 2) * wpp, swoop * 1.2);
    product.scale.setScalar(h * wpp); // the model is normalised to height 1
    // pose: slerp between the two anchors' angles, whole turns on the way in, a slow idle sway, the intro  
spin
    q.slerpQuaternions(poses[i].q, poses[j].q, e);
    qSpin.setFromAxisAngle(Y, (poses[j].spin * e + fall * 1.5) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.sin(t * 0.6) * 0.18);
    q.multiply(qSpin);
    ptr.x += (ptr.tx - ptr.x) * 0.06;
    ptr.y += (ptr.ty - ptr.y) * 0.06;
    lean += (clamp(scrollVelocity() * 0.004, -0.3, 0.3) - lean) * 0.08; // fast scroll: it leans, then  
settles
    qTilt.setFromEuler(tiltE.set(ptr.y * 0.3 + lean, ptr.x * 0.5, 0));
    product.quaternion.copy(qTilt).multiply(q);
    // CSS --dock (0..1) on every anchor: how much the product is "standing" there — drives the floor  
shadow
    for (let k = 0; k <= last; k++) {
      const w = k === i ? 1 - e : k === j ? e : 0;
      const v = Math.round(w * drop.p * 50) / 50;
      if (v !== dock[k]) { dock[k] = v; anchors[k].style.setProperty('--dock', v); }
    }
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
  };
  loadGLB(model).then((gltf) => {
    if (disposed) return;
    product.add(normalizeModel(gltf.scene, { height: 1 }));
    stopLoop = loopWhenVisible(root, frame);
    frame(0, 0);
    isl.live();
    if (intro) introTween = gsap.to(drop, { p: 1, duration: 1.8, ease: 'power3.out', delay: 0.25 });
  }).catch((err) => isl.fail(err));
  return () => {
    disposed = true;
    removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);

    introTween?.kill();
    stopLoop();
    stopSize();
    anchors.forEach((a) => a.style.removeProperty('--dock'));
    disposeRenderer(renderer, scene);
    isl.dispose();
  };
}

element.css
/* E2-02 · Object stage: a 3D product on a round plinth inside a normal section — auto-turn, drag with  
inertia, arrow keys. */
.os {
  display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1.3fr); align-items: center; gap: 
clamp(24px, 4vw, 64px);
  min-block-size: 92svh; padding: 8vh var(--pad, 48px);
  background: radial-gradient(55% 75% at 70% 46%, #F2F5F6 0%, #DCE2E5 62%, #D0D8DC 100%); color: #1D2A33;
}
.os:dir(rtl) { background: radial-gradient(55% 75% at 30% 46%, #F2F5F6 0%, #DCE2E5 62%, #D0D8DC 100%); }
.os__view { position: relative; aspect-ratio: 1 / 0.92; inline-size: 100%; cursor: grab; touch-action: 
pan-y; user-select: none; -webkit-user-select: none; border-radius: 24px; }
.os__view.is-grabbing { cursor: grabbing; }
.os__view:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid #E2572B; outline-offset: -3px; }
.os__canvas, .os__poster { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; }
.os__poster { padding: 10% 16% 20%; object-fit: contain; transition: opacity 0.5s; }
/* still version: a CSS plinth under the poster */
.os__view::before {
  content: ''; position: absolute; inset-inline: 17%; inset-block-end: 15%; block-size: 9%; border-radius: 
50%;
  background: #F3F5F6; box-shadow: 0 7px 0 #C9D1D6, 0 22px 30px rgba(29, 42, 51, 0.16); transition: 
opacity 0.5s;
}
.os__canvas { opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.8s; }
.os[data-gl='live'] .os__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.os[data-gl='live'] .os__poster, .os[data-gl='live'] .os__view::before { opacity: 0; }
.os__hint {
  position: absolute; inset-block-end: 3%; inset-inline: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; 
inline-size: fit-content; margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 8px 15px; border-radius: 999px; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.72); color: #1D2A33; font-
size: 14px; font-weight: 600;
  opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.5s; pointer-events: none;
}
.os[data-gl='live'] .os__hint { opacity: 1; transition-delay: 1.2s; }

element.js
// E2-02 · Object stage — the product as a real 3D object on a round plinth, inside an ordinary page  
section.
// It turns slowly by itself; a horizontal drag (mouse or finger) spins it with inertia; arrow keys turn  
it too;
// scrolling past the section adds part of a turn. Studio light without downloading an HDRI  
(RoomEnvironment), a
// contact-shadow texture instead of shadow maps. Any GLB fits: it is scaled to stand on the plinth.
// The poster (a cut-out image of the same product) is the no-WebGL version. Reduced motion: no auto-turn  
and no
// scroll turn — dragging still works (the visitor starts it).
import { MQ, ScrollTrigger, clamp } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { THREE, island, createRenderer, studioEnvironment, sizeToElement, loopWhenVisible, loadGLB, 
normalizeModel, contactShadowTexture, disposeRenderer } from '@/motion-core/three-kit.js';
export function init(root, { model = '/assets/models/sheen-chair.glb', plinth = '#E9EDEF', autoSpeed = 
0.22, scrollTurn = 0.5, startYaw = -0.55 } = {}) {
  const view = root.querySelector('.os__view');
  const hint = root.querySelector('.os__hint');
  const isl = island(root, { minTier: 2 });
  if (!isl.ok) return isl.dispose;
  const reduce = matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches;
  const auto = reduce ? 0 : autoSpeed;
  let renderer;
  try {
    renderer = createRenderer(isl.canvas, { alpha: true, tier: isl.tier });
  } catch (err) { isl.fail(err); return isl.dispose; }
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  studioEnvironment(renderer, scene, 0.85);
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 2);
  key.position.set(-2.5, 4, 3);
  scene.add(key);
  // spin state: yaw follows drag / inertia / auto-turn; scrollYaw eases toward the scroll target
  let yaw = startYaw, vel = auto, dragging = false, lastX = 0, lastT = 0, touched = false;


## Page 294

תלת־ממדית  מוצר  במת· E2-02תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
294
  let scrollYaw = 0, scrollTarget = 0, dirty = true, disposed = false, stopLoop = () => {};
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(26, 1, 0.1, 50);
  const target = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.42, 0);
  const frameCamera = () => {
    // narrow (portrait) boxes: step back so the plinth still fits the width
    const dist = 6.2 / Math.min(1, camera.aspect / 1.05) ** 0.85;
    camera.position.set(0, 1.75, dist);
    camera.lookAt(target);
  };
  const stopSize = sizeToElement(renderer, camera, view, () => { frameCamera(); dirty = true; });
  const plinthMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.1, 1.14, 0.14, 96), new 
THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: plinth, roughness: 0.6 }));
  plinthMesh.position.y = -0.07;
  const shadow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: 
contactShadowTexture(256, 0.62), transparent: true, depthWrite: false }));
  shadow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  shadow.position.y = 0.003;
  const turntable = new THREE.Group();
  turntable.rotation.y = startYaw;
  scene.add(plinthMesh, shadow, turntable);
  const onDown = (e) => {
    if (e.button > 0) return;
    dragging = true; lastX = e.clientX; lastT = e.timeStamp; vel = 0;
    view.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    view.classList.add('is-grabbing');
  };
  const onMove = (e) => {
    if (!dragging) return;
    const k = 6 / Math.max(320, view.clientWidth); // one full turn ≈  the width of the stage
    const dx = (e.clientX - lastX) * k;
    yaw += dx;
    vel = clamp(dx / Math.max(1 / 120, (e.timeStamp - lastT) / 1000), -9, 9);
    lastX = e.clientX; lastT = e.timeStamp; dirty = true;
    if (!touched && Math.abs(dx) > 0.02) { touched = true; hint?.classList.add('is-gone'); }
  };
  const onUp = () => { dragging = false; view.classList.remove('is-grabbing'); };
  const onKey = (e) => {
    const dir = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0;
    if (!dir) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    vel += dir * 2.4; dirty = true;
    if (!touched) { touched = true; hint?.classList.add('is-gone'); }
  };
  view.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
  view.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
  view.addEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
  view.addEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);
  view.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  const st = reduce ? null : ScrollTrigger.create({
    trigger: root, start: 'top bottom' , end: 'bottom top' ,
    onUpdate: (self) => { scrollTarget = (self.progress - 0.5) * scrollTurn * Math.PI; },
  });
  const frame = (dt) => {
    if (!dragging) {
      vel += (auto - vel) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.5); // inertia settles back to the slow auto-turn
      yaw += vel * dt;
    }
    const ds = (scrollTarget - scrollYaw) * Math.min(1, dt * 5);
    scrollYaw += ds;
    if (!dirty && Math.abs(vel) < 1e-4 && Math.abs(ds) < 1e-5) return; // nothing moved: skip the frame
    turntable.rotation.y = yaw + scrollYaw;
    renderer.render(scene, camera);


## Page 295

תלת־ממדית  מוצר  במת· E2-02תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
295
    dirty = false;
  };
  loadGLB(model).then((gltf) => {
    if (disposed) return;
    const obj = normalizeModel(gltf.scene, { height: 1 });
    // fit any product on the plinth: footprint ≤  the plinth, then stand it on top
    const size = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(obj).getSize(new THREE.Vector3());
    const k = Math.min(1, 1.9 / Math.hypot(size.x, size.z));
    obj.scale.setScalar(k);
    obj.position.y = (size.y * k) / 2;
    turntable.add(obj);
    stopLoop = loopWhenVisible(view, frame);
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
    view.tabIndex = 0; // keyboard turning only exists in 3D
    isl.live();
  }).catch((err) => isl.fail(err));
  return () => {
    disposed = true;
    stopLoop();
    stopSize();
    st?.kill();
    view.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
    view.removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
    view.removeEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
    view.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);
    view.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    view.removeAttribute('tabindex');
    disposeRenderer(renderer, scene);
    isl.dispose();
  };
}

element.css
/* E2-03 · Hotspot & swatch viewer: 3D product + colour swatches (radio buttons) + info buttons glued to  
points on the model. */
.hs {
  display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(0, 1.45fr); align-items: center; gap: 
clamp(24px, 4vw, 64px);
  min-block-size: 92svh; padding: 8vh var(--pad, 48px); background: #E4E9E3; color: #17201B;
}
.hs__copy { max-inline-size: 28rem; justify-self: center; }

מידע  ונקודות  דוגמיות  עם  מוצר· E2-03תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
298
.hs__title { margin: 0; font: 800 clamp(38px, 4.6vw, 74px)/1.02 var(--font-round); letter-spacing: 
-0.02em; text-wrap: balance; }
.hs__copy > p { margin: 16px 0 0; color: #47544B; font-size: clamp(17px, 1.35vw, 20px); }
/* swatches: native radios, the circle is the label */
.hs__swatches { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 14px; margin: 30px 0 0; padding: 0; border: 0; }
.hs__swatches legend { margin-bottom: 12px; padding: 0; color: #47544B; font-size: 15px; }
.hs__chosen { color: #17201B; font-weight: 700; }
.hs__sw { position: relative; display: block; cursor: pointer; }
.hs__sw input { position: absolute; inset: 0; margin: 0; opacity: 0; cursor: pointer; }
.hs__chip {
  display: block; inline-size: 42px; block-size: 42px; border-radius: 50%;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--chip) 0 62%, var(--chip2, var(--chip)) 62%);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.14); outline: 2px solid transparent; outline-offset: 3px; 
transition: outline-color 0.2s;
}
.hs__sw input:checked + .hs__chip { outline-color: #17201B; }
.hs__sw input:focus-visible + .hs__chip { outline: 3px solid #F0505A; }
.hs__swname, .hs__dot-label { position: absolute; inline-size: 1px; block-size: 1px; overflow: hidden; 
clip-path: inset(50%); white-space: nowrap; }
.hs__copy > .hs__price { margin-top: 26px; color: #17201B; font: 700 clamp(28px, 2.4vw, 38px)/1 var(--
font-sans); }
.hs__cta { display: inline-block; margin-top: 22px; padding: 15px 30px; border-radius: 999px; background: 
#17201B; color: #F1F4EF; font-weight: 800; text-decoration: none; }
.hs__cta:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid #F0505A; outline-offset: 3px; }
/* stage: the poster keeps the height (also in 3D mode, invisible), canvas and hotspots sit on top */
.hs__stage { position: relative; cursor: grab; touch-action: pan-y; user-select: none; -webkit-user-
select: none; }
.hs__stage.is-grabbing { cursor: grabbing; }
.hs__stage::before { content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 8% 6% 4%; z-index: -1; border-radius: 50%; 
background: radial-gradient(closest-side, #F4F7F3, rgba(244, 247, 243, 0)); }
.hs__poster { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: auto; transition: opacity 0.5s, filter 0.4s; 
}
.hs__canvas { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; opacity: 0; transition: 
opacity 0.8s; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__poster { opacity: 0; }
/* still version (no WebGL / no JS): the hotspot texts are a plain list under the picture */
.hs__spots { display: grid; gap: 14px; margin: 18px 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; }
.hs__dot { display: none; }
.hs__tip strong { display: block; margin-bottom: 2px; }
.hs__tip p { margin: 0; color: #47544B; }
/* 3D mode: every spot is a 0×0 point moved by JS (transform), the dot centred on it, the tip above it */
.hs[data-gl='loading'] .hs__spots, .hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__spots { position: absolute; inset: 0; margin: 
0; pointer-events: none; }
.hs[data-gl='loading'] .hs__spots { visibility: hidden; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__spot { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; inline-size: 0; block-size: 0; 
pointer-events: auto; transition: opacity 0.25s; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__dot {
  position: absolute; left: -17px; top: -17px; display: grid; place-items: center; inline-size: 34px; 
block-size: 34px; padding: 0;
  border: 0; border-radius: 50%; background: #fff; color: #17201B; cursor: pointer;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 5px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.38), 0 6px 18px rgba(23, 32, 27, 0.25); transition: 
transform 0.25s, background-color 0.2s;
}
.hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__dot::before, .hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__dot::after { content: ''; grid-area: 1 / 1; 
inline-size: 13px; block-size: 2px; border-radius: 2px; background: currentColor; transition: rotate 
0.25s; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .hs__dot::after { rotate: 90deg; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .is-open .hs__dot { background: #17201B; color: #fff; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .is-open .hs__dot::before { rotate: 45deg; }
.hs[data-gl='live'] .is-open .hs__dot::after { rotate: 135deg; }

// E2-03 · Hotspot & swatch viewer — a 3D product with colour swatches and information points ON the  
model.
// Swatches are native radio buttons. A colour is either a variant stored in the GLB  
(KHR_materials_variants) or a
// runtime recolour of another variant's texture (canvas filter: data-from + data-filter) — one model, any  
colour.
// Changing colour makes the product hop and spin once while the material swaps. Hotspots are real  
<button>s
// glued to 3D points (data-pos, model space) by projecting them every frame; they fade when their side  
turns away
// (data-normal). Opening one turns the product to face it. Drag turns it with inertia; it idles slowly  
otherwise.
// Still version: the poster per colour (same camera) and the hotspot texts as a plain list.
import { gsap, MQ, clamp } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { THREE, island, createRenderer, studioEnvironment, sizeToElement, loopWhenVisible, loadGLB, 
normalizeModel, contactShadowTexture, materialVariants, disposeRenderer } from '@/motion-core/three-
kit.js';
const vec = (s, d = [0, 0, 0]) => new THREE.Vector3(...(s ? s.split(',').map(Number) : d));
const smooth = (a, b, x) => { const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a)); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
// recolour a material by drawing its colour texture through a CSS filter (cached per material + filter)
const tintCache = new Map();
function tinted(mat, filter) {
  const key = mat.uuid + filter;
  if (tintCache.has(key)) return tintCache.get(key);
  const m = mat.clone();
  const src = mat.map;
  if (src?.image) {
    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = src.image.width; c.height = src.image.height;
    const g = c.getContext('2d');
    g.filter = filter;
    g.drawImage(src.image, 0, 0);
    const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);

מידע  ונקודות  דוגמיות  עם  מוצר· E2-03תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
300
    ['colorSpace', 'flipY', 'wrapS', 'wrapT', 'channel', 'anisotropy', 'rotation'].forEach((k) => { t[k] = 
src[k]; });
    t.offset.copy(src.offset); t.repeat.copy(src.repeat); t.center.copy(src.center);
    m.map = t;
  }
  tintCache.set(key, m);
  return m;
}
export function init(root, { model = '/assets/models/materials-variants-shoe.glb', poster = (v) => 
`/assets/img/shoe-${v}.webp`, autoSpeed = 0.2, startYaw = 0.32 } = {}) {
  const stage = root.querySelector('.hs__stage');
  const img = root.querySelector('.hs__poster');
  const chosen = root.querySelector('.hs__chosen');
  const radios = [...root.querySelectorAll('.hs__swatches input' )];
  const spots = [...root.querySelectorAll('.hs__spot')].map((el) => ({
    el, dot: el.querySelector('.hs__dot'), tip: el.querySelector('.hs__tip'), pos: vec(el.dataset.pos), 
normal: vec(el.dataset.normal, [0, 0, 1]).normalize(),
  }));
  const reduce = matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches;
  let apply3d = null;
  // swatches work in every mode: the still version swaps the poster (same camera) and applies the same  
filter
  const onPick = (e) => {
    const input = e.target;
    chosen.textContent = input.closest('label').textContent.trim();
    if (apply3d) { apply3d(input); return; }
    img.src = poster(input.dataset.from || input.value);
    img.style.filter = input.dataset.filter || '';
  };
  radios.forEach((r) => r.addEventListener('change', onPick));
  const offPick = () => radios.forEach((r) => r.removeEventListener('change', onPick));
  const isl = island(root, { minTier: 2 });
  if (!isl.ok) return () => { offPick(); isl.dispose(); };
  let renderer;
  try {
    renderer = createRenderer(isl.canvas, { alpha: true, tier: isl.tier });
  } catch (err) { isl.fail(err); return () => { offPick(); isl.dispose(); }; }
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  studioEnvironment(renderer, scene, 0.9);
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.8);
  key.position.set(-2, 4, 3);
  scene.add(key);
  const S = { yaw: startYaw, spin: 0, hop: 0 };
  let W = 1, H = 1, vel = reduce ? 0 : autoSpeed, dragging = false, lastX = 0, lastT = 0, open = null, 
faceTween = null, hopTl = null;
  let disposed = false, stopLoop = () => {}, hovering = false, calm = 0;
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(24, 1, 0.1, 50);
  const stopSize = sizeToElement(renderer, camera, stage, (w, h) => {
    W = w; H = h;
    const dist = 4.3 / Math.min(1, camera.aspect / 1.15) ** 0.9; // narrow stage: step back so the length  
fits
    camera.position.set(0, 1.05, dist);
    camera.lookAt(0, -0.04, 0);
  });
  const turntable = new THREE.Group();
  const shadow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.6, 1.3), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: 
contactShadowTexture(256, 0.5), transparent: true, depthWrite: false }));
  shadow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  shadow.position.y = -0.56;
  scene.add(turntable, shadow);


## Page 301

מידע  ונקודות  דוגמיות  עם  מוצר· E2-03תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
301
  let wrap = null, variants = null;
  const applyColor = async (input) => {
    await variants.select(input.dataset.from || input.value);
    const filter = input.dataset.filter;
    if (filter) wrap.traverse((o) => { if (o.isMesh) o.material = tinted(o.material, filter); });
  };
  apply3d = (input) => {
    if (reduce) { applyColor(input); return; }
    hopTl?.kill();
    hopTl = gsap.timeline()
      .to(S, { hop: 1, duration: 0.42, ease: 'power2.out' })
      .add(() => { applyColor(input); })        // swap at the top of the jump, mid-spin
      .to(S, { hop: 0, duration: 0.7, ease: 'bounce.out' });
    gsap.to(S, { spin: Math.round(S.spin / (Math.PI * 2) + 1) * Math.PI * 2, duration: 1.1, ease: 
'power2.inOut' });
  };
  // hotspots: open one at a time; the product turns to face it
  const setOpen = (spot) => {
    spots.forEach((s) => {
      const on = s === spot;
      s.el.classList.toggle('is-open', on);
      s.dot.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(on));
    });
    open = spot;
    if (!spot) return;
    const target = -Math.atan2(spot.normal.x, spot.normal.z) - S.spin; // azimuth of the normal →  facing  
the camera
    const turn = target + Math.round((S.yaw - target) / (Math.PI * 2)) * Math.PI * 2;   // nearest  
equivalent angle
    vel = 0;
    faceTween?.kill();
    faceTween = gsap.to(S, { yaw: turn, duration: reduce ? 0 : 0.9, ease: 'power3.inOut' });
  };
  const onDot = (e) => {
    const spot = spots.find((s) => s.dot === e.currentTarget);
    setOpen(open === spot ? null : spot);
  };
  const onKey = (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape' && open) { const d = open.dot; setOpen(null); d.focus(); } 
};
  const onOutside = (e) => { if (open && !e.target.closest('.hs__spot')) setOpen(null); };
  spots.forEach((s) => s.dot.addEventListener('click', onDot));
  root.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown', onOutside);
  // drag to turn (not when the press starts on a hotspot)
  const onDown = (e) => {
    if (e.button > 0 || e.target.closest('.hs__spot')) return;
    dragging = true; lastX = e.clientX; lastT = e.timeStamp; vel = 0;
    faceTween?.kill();
    stage.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    stage.classList.add('is-grabbing');
  };
  const onMove = (e) => {
    if (!dragging) return;
    const dx = ((e.clientX - lastX) * 6) / Math.max(320, stage.clientWidth);
    S.yaw += dx;
    vel = clamp(dx / Math.max(1 / 120, (e.timeStamp - lastT) / 1000), -9, 9);
    lastX = e.clientX; lastT = e.timeStamp;
  };
  const onUp = () => { dragging = false; stage.classList.remove('is-grabbing'); };
  // a mouse over the stage holds the product still, so the hotspots are easy to hit (moving targets are  
hard)
  const onEnter = (e) => { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') hovering = true; };

מידע  ונקודות  דוגמיות  עם  מוצר· E2-03תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
302
  const onLeave = () => { hovering = false; };
  stage.addEventListener('pointerenter', onEnter);
  stage.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
  stage.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
  stage.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
  stage.addEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
  stage.addEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);
  const p = new THREE.Vector3(), n = new THREE.Vector3(), toCam = new THREE.Vector3();
  const frame = (dt, t) => {
    calm += ((open || hovering || reduce ? 1 : 0) - calm) * Math.min(1, dt * 3); // open hotspot / mouse  
over: hold still
    if (!dragging && !faceTween?.isActive()) {
      vel += (autoSpeed * (1 - calm) - vel) * Math.min(1, dt * (hovering ? 4 : 1.5));
      S.yaw += vel * dt;
    }
    const bob = Math.sin(t * 1.4) * 0.025 * (1 - calm);
    turntable.rotation.y = S.yaw + S.spin;
    turntable.position.y = S.hop * 0.32 + bob;
    shadow.material.opacity = 1 - S.hop * 0.6 - bob * 4;
    turntable.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    for (const s of spots) {
      p.copy(s.pos);
      wrap.localToWorld(p);
      n.copy(s.normal).transformDirection(wrap.matrixWorld);
      const facing = n.dot(toCam.copy(camera.position).sub(p).normalize());
      p.project(camera);
      s.el.style.transform = `translate(${((p.x + 1) / 2) * W}px, ${((1 - p.y) / 2) * H}px)`;
      const vis = s === open ? 1 : smooth(-0.1, 0.25, facing);
      s.el.style.opacity = vis.toFixed(2);
      s.el.classList.toggle('is-back', vis < 0.35);
    }
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
  };
  loadGLB(model).then(async (gltf) => {
    if (disposed) return;
    wrap = normalizeModel(gltf.scene, { height: 1 });
    variants = materialVariants(gltf);
    const picked = radios.find((r) => r.checked);
    if (picked) await applyColor(picked);
    turntable.add(wrap);
    stopLoop = loopWhenVisible(stage, frame);
    frame(0, 0);
    isl.live();
  }).catch((err) => isl.fail(err));
  return () => {
    disposed = true;
    offPick();
    stopLoop();
    stopSize();
    faceTween?.kill();
    hopTl?.kill();
    gsap.killTweensOf(S);
    spots.forEach((s) => { s.dot.removeEventListener('click', onDot); s.el.style.transform = ''; 
s.el.style.opacity = ''; });
    root.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onOutside);
    stage.removeEventListener('pointerenter', onEnter);
    stage.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    stage.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
    stage.removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
    stage.removeEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
    stage.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);

    tintCache.clear();
    disposeRenderer(renderer, scene);
    isl.dispose();
  };
}

/* E2-04 · Exploded 3D: a pinned section — the model turns, splits into named layers with leader lines,  
and closes again. */
.xw {
  position: relative; display: grid; grid-template-rows: auto 1fr; min-block-size: 100svh; padding: 9vh 
var(--pad, 48px) 3vh; overflow: hidden;
  background: radial-gradient(70% 60% at 50% 60%, #23211E 0%, #131313 60%, #0B0B0C 100%); color: #EDE6D6;
}
.xw[data-gl='loading'], .xw[data-gl='live'] { block-size: 100svh; }
.xw__head { position: relative; z-index: 2; max-inline-size: 34rem; }
.xw__title { margin: 0; font: 500 clamp(34px, 4.2vw, 66px)/1.05 var(--font-serif); text-wrap: balance; }
.xw__head p { margin: 12px 0 0; color: #A79F90; font-size: clamp(16px, 1.3vw, 19px); }
.xw__stage { position: relative; min-block-size: 0; }
/* still version (no WebGL / reduced motion / no JS): the exploded picture and the layers as a list */
.xw__poster { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: auto; max-block-size: 62svh; object-fit: 
contain; margin-block-start: 3vh; transition: opacity 0.6s; }
.xw__labels { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr)); gap: 16px 24px; 
margin: 3vh 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; }
.xw__labels strong { display: block; color: #D8B66A; font-size: 16px; }
.xw__labels span { color: #A79F90; font-size: 14px; }
.xw__canvas, .xw__lines { position: absolute; inset: 0; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; }
.xw__canvas { opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.8s; }
.xw__lines { pointer-events: none; overflow: visible; }
.xw__lines line { stroke: #D8B66A; stroke-width: 1; stroke-linecap: round; stroke-dasharray: 2 4; }
/* 3D mode: the poster becomes a placeholder behind the canvas, the labels follow their layers */
.xw[data-gl='loading'] .xw__poster, .xw[data-gl='live'] .xw__poster { position: absolute; inset: 0; 
inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; max-block-size: none; margin: 0; }
.xw[data-gl='live'] .xw__poster { opacity: 0; }
.xw[data-gl='live'] .xw__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.xw[data-gl='loading'] .xw__labels, .xw[data-gl='live'] .xw__labels { position: absolute; inset: 0; 
display: block; margin: 0; pointer-events: none; }
.xw[data-gl='loading'] .xw__labels { visibility: hidden; }
.xw[data-gl='live'] .xw__labels li { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; inline-size: max-content; max-
inline-size: 170px; text-align: center; opacity: 0; }
.xw[data-gl='live'] .xw__labels strong { font-size: 15px; }
.xw[data-gl='live'] .xw__labels span { font-size: 13px; }

element.js
// E2-04 · Exploded 3D — a pinned section where scrolling turns a real 3D product to three quarters, pulls  
it apart
// along its own axis into named layers (each layer = the meshes whose name starts with data-part, moved  
by
// data-offset along the model's +Z), shows a label with a leader line for each layer, then puts it back  
together.
// Landscape screens explode sideways (labels above/below), portrait screens explode upward (labels  
left/right).
// The model's own animation (the seconds hand) ticks once a second; frames are drawn only when something  
changed. Still version (no WebGL / reduced motion): an exploded
// render of the same model + the labels as a list.
import { gsap, MQ, clamp } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { THREE, island, createRenderer, studioEnvironment, sizeToElement, loopWhenVisible, loadGLB, 
normalizeModel, disposeRenderer } from '@/motion-core/three-kit.js';
const smooth = (a, b, x) => { const t = clamp((x - a) / (b - a)); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
const SVGNS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
export function init(root, { model = '/assets/models/chronograph-watch.glb', length = 2.5 } = {}) {
  const stage = root.querySelector('.xw__stage');
  const svg = root.querySelector('.xw__lines');
  const items = [...root.querySelectorAll('.xw__labels li' )].map((li, i) => ({
    li, i, key: li.dataset.part, offset: Number(li.dataset.offset || 0), meshes: [], center: new 
THREE.Vector3(), e: 0,
  }));
  const isl = island(root, { minTier: 2 });
  if (!isl.ok || matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches) { isl.fail(); return isl.dispose; }
  let renderer;
  try {
    renderer = createRenderer(isl.canvas, { alpha: true, tier: isl.tier });
  } catch (err) { isl.fail(err); return isl.dispose; }
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  studioEnvironment(renderer, scene, 1);
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff4e0, 2.4);
  key.position.set(-3, 3, 4);
  const rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1.6);
  rim.position.set(4, 1, -3);
  scene.add(key, rim);
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(30, 1, 0.1, 50);
  let W = 1, H = 1, portrait = false, lastP = -1, lastSec = -1, dirty = true; // before sizeToElement: it  
calls back at once
  const stopSize = sizeToElement(renderer, camera, stage, (w, h) => {
    W = w; H = h; portrait = h > w * 1.1; dirty = true;
    svg.setAttribute('viewBox', `0 0 ${w} ${h}`);
    // fit: landscape needs room for the sideways stack, portrait for its width plus labels
    const fitW = (portrait ? 1.7 : 3.3) / (camera.aspect * 2 * Math.tan(THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(15)));
    camera.position.set(0, 0, Math.max(portrait ? 4.4 : 3.5, fitW));
    camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
  });
  const turntable = new THREE.Group();
  scene.add(turntable);
  const lines = items.map(() => { const l = document.createElementNS(SVGNS, 'line'); svg.append(l); return 
l; });
  const state = { p: 0 };


## Page 307

בגלילה  תלת־ממדי  פיצוץ· E2-04תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
307
  let wrap = null, mixer = null, tween = null, stopLoop = () => {}, disposed = false;
  const a = new THREE.Vector3();
  const frame = () => {
    // draw only when something changed: the scroll position, or the seconds hand (it ticks once a second,  
like a
    // quartz watch — and it shows the real seconds). Idle cost: one frame per second.
    const p = state.p, sec = new Date().getSeconds();
    if (p === lastP && sec === lastSec && !dirty) return;
    lastP = p; lastSec = sec; dirty = false;
    mixer?.setTime(sec);
    const turn = smooth(0, 0.2, p) * (1 - smooth(0.88, 1, p));
    let sum = 0;
    for (const it of items) {
      it.e = smooth(0.16 + it.i * 0.05, 0.46 + it.i * 0.05, p) * (1 - smooth(0.76 + it.i * 0.02, 0.9, p));
      sum += it.e;
      for (const m of it.meshes) m.o.position.lerpVectors(m.base, m.target, it.e);
    }
    wrap.position.z = -0.42 * (sum / items.length); // keep the growing stack centred
    if (portrait) turntable.rotation.set(-1.05 * turn, 0.35 * turn, 0, 'YXZ'); // explode upward
    else turntable.rotation.set(0.18 * turn, -1.0 * turn, 0, 'YXZ');           // explode sideways
    turntable.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    // labels: project each layer's centre (moved with it) and park the label beside the stack
    for (const it of items) {
      a.copy(it.center);
      a.z += it.offset * it.e;
      wrap.localToWorld(a).project(camera);
      const ax = ((a.x + 1) / 2) * W, ay = ((1 - a.y) / 2) * H;
      const side = it.i % 2 ? 1 : -1;
      const lx = portrait ? clamp(ax + side * W * 0.3, 70, W - 70) : ax;
      const ly = portrait ? ay : clamp(ay + side * H * 0.27, 60, H - 50);
      const o = smooth(0.72, 1, it.e);
      it.li.style.transform = `translate(${lx}px, ${ly}px) translate(-50%, -50%)` ;
      it.li.style.opacity = o.toFixed(2);
      const l = lines[it.i];
      const gap = portrait ? 0 : side * 26; // stop the line short of the label text
      l.setAttribute('x1', ax.toFixed(1)); l.setAttribute('y1', ay.toFixed(1));
      l.setAttribute('x2', lx.toFixed(1)); l.setAttribute('y2', (ly - gap).toFixed(1));
      l.style.opacity = o.toFixed(2);
    }
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
  };
  // pin right away (not after the model loads) so the page length never jumps under the visitor
  tween = gsap.to(state, {
    p: 1, ease: 'none',
    scrollTrigger: { trigger: root, start: 'top top' , end: `+=${length * 100}%`, pin: true, scrub: 0.8 },
  });
  loadGLB(model).then((gltf) => {
    if (disposed) return;
    wrap = normalizeModel(gltf.scene, { height: 1 });
    wrap.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    // glass with transmission needs an extra full render per frame — a thin reflective disc reads the  
same here
    wrap.traverse((o) => {
      if (o.isMesh && o.material.transmission > 0) Object.assign(o.material, { transmission: 0, 
transparent: true, opacity: 0.22, roughness: 0.05 });
    });
    // every layer: its meshes' rest and exploded positions (computed while the model is still at the  
origin)
    const box = new THREE.Box3();
    for (const it of items) {
      box.makeEmpty();
      wrap.traverse((o) => {


## Page 308

בגלילה  תלת־ממדי  פיצוץ· E2-04תלת־ממד  רגעי:  2 טייר —  20 פרק
308
        if (!o.isMesh || !o.name.startsWith(it.key)) return;
        // a new vector per mesh: worldToLocal() writes into the vector it is given (a shared temp = every  
part
        // would end up with the last part's target)
        const target = o.parent.worldToLocal(o.getWorldPosition(new THREE.Vector3()).add(new 
THREE.Vector3(0, 0, it.offset)));
        it.meshes.push({ o, base: o.position.clone(), target });
        box.expandByObject(o);
      });
      if (!box.isEmpty()) box.getCenter(it.center);
    }
    turntable.add(wrap);
    if (gltf.animations.length) {
      mixer = new THREE.AnimationMixer(gltf.scene);
      gltf.animations.forEach((clip) => mixer.clipAction(clip).play());
    }
    stopLoop = loopWhenVisible(root, frame);
    frame();
    isl.live();
  }).catch((err) => { tween?.scrollTrigger?.kill(); tween?.kill(); tween = null; isl.fail(err); });
  return () => {
    disposed = true;
    stopLoop();
    stopSize();
    tween?.scrollTrigger?.kill();
    tween?.kill();
    mixer?.stopAllAction();
    lines.forEach((l) => l.remove());
    items.forEach((it) => { it.li.style.transform = ''; it.li.style.opacity = ''; });
    disposeRenderer(renderer, scene);
    isl.dispose();
  };
}

element.css
/* E2-05 · Logo extrude: the inline SVG logo becomes bevelled 3D pieces that fly in and catch moving  
reflections. */
.lx {
  display: grid; justify-items: center; align-content: center; gap: 14px; min-block-size: 100svh; padding: 
8vh var(--pad, 48px); overflow: hidden; text-align: center;
  background: radial-gradient(60% 55% at 50% 36%, #1B3150 0%, #0F1D31 55%, #0B1524 100%); color: #EEE7DA;
}
.lx__logo { position: relative; inline-size: min(44vmin, 400px); aspect-ratio: 1; margin-block-end: 3vh; }
.lx__logo svg { display: block; inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; overflow: visible; filter: drop-
shadow(0 18px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45)); transition: opacity 0.6s; }
/* the canvas is larger than the logo box so the turning pieces never clip */
.lx__canvas { position: absolute; inset: -35%; inline-size: 170%; block-size: 170%; max-inline-size: none; 
opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.6s; pointer-events: none; }
.lx[data-gl='live'] .lx__canvas { opacity: 1; }
.lx[data-gl='live'] .lx__logo svg { opacity: 0; }
.lx__title { margin: 0; font: 500 clamp(40px, 5.4vw, 84px)/1 var(--font-serif); letter-spacing: -0.01em; }
.lx__text { margin: 0; max-inline-size: 36ch; color: #B7B0A3; font-size: clamp(17px, 1.4vw, 20px); }
.lx__cta { margin-block-start: 14px; padding: 15px 30px; border-radius: 999px; background: #C99A4B; color: 
#0B1524; font-weight: 800; text-decoration: none; }
.lx__cta:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid #EEE7DA; outline-offset: 3px; }
element.js
// E2-05 · Logo extrude — the logo's own inline <svg> (the real, accessible content) is parsed with  
SVGLoader and

/* E1-57 · Pricing switch: monthly/yearly segmented switch; prices roll to the new value; live border on  
the best plan. */
.ps { padding: 10vh var(--pad, 48px); background: #EEF2F7; color: #101828; text-align: center; }
.ps__title { margin-inline: auto; max-inline-size: 18ch; font: 700 clamp(32px, 3.8vw, 58px)/1.04 var(--
font-sans, system-ui); letter-spacing: -0.02em; }
.ps__switch { position: relative; display: inline-flex; margin: 28px auto 40px; padding: 5px; border-
radius: 999px; background: #fff; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(16, 24, 40, 0.08); }
.ps__switch button { position: relative; z-index: 1; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; 
padding: 11px 22px; border: 0; border-radius: 999px; background: none; color: #475467; font-weight: 
600; cursor: pointer; transition: color 0.3s; }
.ps__switch button[aria-checked='true'] { color: #fff; }
.ps__switch button:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #3E5BF2; outline-offset: 2px; }
.ps__thumb { position: absolute; top: 5px; bottom: 5px; left: 0; border-radius: 999px; background: 
#101828; }
.ps__save { padding: 3px 8px; border-radius: 999px; background: #D7F5E3; color: #0B6B3A; font-size: 12px; 
font-weight: 700; }
.ps__sr { position: absolute; inline-size: 1px; block-size: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip-path: inset(50%); 
white-space: nowrap; }
.ps__plans { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: 18px; align-items: 
stretch; max-inline-size: 1180px; margin-inline: auto; text-align: start; }

.ps__card { position: relative; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; padding: 30px 26px; 
border-radius: 24px; background: #fff; border: 2px solid transparent; }
.ps__card h3 { font: 700 24px/1.2 var(--font-sans, system-ui); }
.ps__for { color: #667085; }
.ps__price { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 2px; margin-top: 14px; direction: ltr; justify-
content: flex-end; font: 800 60px/1 var(--font-round, system-ui); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; 
}
.ps__price:dir(ltr) { justify-content: flex-start; }
.ps__cur { margin-top: 6px; font-size: 26px; }
/* old and new numbers share one grid cell and roll through it */
.ps__amt { display: inline-grid; overflow: clip; padding-block: 4px; }
.ps__amt > span { grid-area: 1 / 1; }
.ps__per { min-block-size: 1.6em; color: #667085; font-size: 14px; }
.ps__card ul { display: grid; gap: 8px; margin: 18px 0 24px; padding: 0; list-style: none; }
.ps__card li::before { content: ' ✓ '; margin-inline-end: 8px; color: #3E5BF2; font-weight: 800; }
.ps__cta { margin-top: auto; padding: 13px; border-radius: 14px; background: #EEF2F7; color: #101828; 
font-weight: 700; text-align: center; text-decoration: none; }
.ps__cta:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #3E5BF2; outline-offset: 2px; }
@property --ps-a { syntax: '<angle>'; inherits: false; initial-value: 0deg; }
.ps__card--best {
  background: linear-gradient(#101828, #101828) padding-box,
    conic-gradient(from var(--ps-a), #3E5BF2, #9FD3FF, #3E5BF2 35%, #101828 55%, #3E5BF2) border-box;
  color: #F2F4F7; animation: ps-spin 6s linear infinite; transform: translateY(-10px);
  box-shadow: 0 30px 60px -30px rgba(62, 91, 242, 0.55);
}
@keyframes ps-spin { to { --ps-a: 360deg; } }
.ps__card--best .ps__for, .ps__card--best .ps__per { color: #98A2B3; }
.ps__card--best li::before { color: #9FD3FF; }
.ps__card--best .ps__cta { background: #3E5BF2; color: #fff; }
.ps__tag { position: absolute; top: -14px; inset-inline-start: 24px; padding: 5px 12px; border-radius: 
999px; background: #9FD3FF; color: #101828; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 800; }
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .ps__plans { grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr); max-inline-size: 460px; }
  .ps__card--best { order: -1; transform: none; margin-top: 14px; }
}
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { .ps__card--best { animation: none; } }
element.js
// E1-57 · Pricing switch — a monthly/yearly radiogroup (arrow keys work) with a sliding thumb.
// On change every price "rolls": the old number slides up and out, the new one rises from below,
// card by card. The note under each price swaps the same way. One polite live message for screen readers.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root) {
  const radios = [...root.querySelectorAll('[role="radio"]')];
  const thumb = root.querySelector('.ps__thumb');
  const amts = [...root.querySelectorAll('.ps__amt')];
  const pers = [...root.querySelectorAll('.ps__per')];
  const live = root.querySelector('.ps__sr');
  const reduce = () => matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches;
  let period = radios.find((r) => r.getAttribute('aria-checked') === 'true')?.dataset.period || 
radios[0].dataset.period;
  // wrap current values in a span so old and new can overlap
  [...amts, ...pers].forEach((el) => { el.innerHTML = `<span>${el.dataset[period]}</span>`; });
  const placeThumb = (animate) => {
    const r = radios.find((x) => x.dataset.period === period);
    const vars = { x: r.offsetLeft, width: r.offsetWidth };
    animate && !reduce() ? gsap.to(thumb, { ...vars, duration: 0.45, ease: 'power3.inOut' }) : 
gsap.set(thumb, vars);


## Page 236

מתג  עם  מחירון· E1-57תלת־ממד  בלי:  1 טייר —  10 פרק
236
  };
  const roll = (el, text, i) => {
    const old = el.lastElementChild;
    const next = document.createElement('span');
    next.textContent = text;
    el.append(next);
    if (reduce()) { old.remove(); return; }
    gsap.timeline({ delay: i * 0.06, defaults: { overwrite: 'auto' } })
      .to(old, { yPercent: -110, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.32, ease: 'power2.in', onComplete: () => 
old.remove() })
      .fromTo(next, { yPercent: 110, autoAlpha: 0 }, { yPercent: 0, autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.5, ease: 
'back.out(1.6)' }, 0.14);
  };
  const select = (p, focus = false) => {
    if (p === period) return;
    period = p;
    radios.forEach((r) => {
      const on = r.dataset.period === p;
      r.setAttribute('aria-checked', String(on));
      r.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1;
      if (on && focus) r.focus();
    });
    placeThumb(true);
    amts.forEach((el, i) => roll(el, el.dataset[p], i));
    pers.forEach((el, i) => roll(el, el.dataset[p], i));
    if (live) live.textContent = p === 'year' ? 'חודשי  בחיוב  מחירים  מוצגים'  : 'שנתי  בחיוב  מחירים  מוצגים';
  };
  radios.forEach((r) => { r.tabIndex = r.dataset.period === period ? 0 : -1; });
  const onClick = (e) => { const r = e.target.closest('[role="radio"]'); if (r && root.contains(r)) 
select(r.dataset.period); };
  const onKey = (e) => {
    if (!['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown'].includes(e.key)) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const i = radios.findIndex((r) => r.dataset.period === period);
    const step = e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowUp' ? (e.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1)
      : (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') === (getComputedStyle(root).direction === 'rtl') ? 1 : -1; // RTL: left =  
next
    select(radios[(i + step + radios.length) % radios.length].dataset.period, true);
  };
  const group = root.querySelector('[role="radiogroup"]');
  group.addEventListener('click', onClick);
  group.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
  const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => placeThumb(false));
  ro.observe(group);
  document.fonts?.ready.then(() => placeThumb(false));
  return () => {
    group.removeEventListener('click', onClick);
    group.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    ro.disconnect();
    gsap.killTweensOf([thumb]);
    gsap.set(thumb, { clearProps: 'all' });
    [...amts, ...pers].forEach((el) => { el.textContent = el.dataset[period]; });
  };
}

/* E1-58 · Smooth accordion: native <details name> (works without JS) + GSAP height animation and  
exclusivity. */
.ac { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 0.8fr) minmax(0, 1.2fr); gap: 40px 64px; align-
items: start; padding: 11vh var(--pad, 48px); background: #1D2B24; color: #EEF0E8; }
.ac__side { position: sticky; top: 12vh; display: grid; gap: 16px; justify-items: start; }
.ac__title { font: 400 clamp(36px, 4.2vw, 68px)/1.02 var(--font-serif, serif); }
.ac__side p { max-inline-size: 30ch; color: #B9C2B5; font-size: 18px; }
.ac__cta { padding: 13px 24px; border-radius: 999px; background: #E9C46A; color: #1D2B24; font-weight: 
700; text-decoration: none; }
.ac__cta:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #E9C46A; outline-offset: 3px; }
.ac__list { display: grid; gap: 10px; }
.ac__item { border-radius: 18px; background: rgba(238, 240, 232, 0.05); transition: background-color 0.4s; 
}
.ac__item[open]:not(.is-closing) { background: rgba(238, 240, 232, 0.11); }
.ac__item summary {
  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 20px; padding: 22px 24px;
  list-style: none; cursor: pointer; font: 600 clamp(18px, 1.5vw, 22px)/1.35 var(--font-sans, system-ui);
}
.ac__item summary::-webkit-details-marker { display: none; }
.ac__item summary:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid #E9C46A; outline-offset: 2px; border-radius: 18px; }
/* plus that turns into minus: the vertical bar folds away */
.ac__icon { position: relative; flex: none; inline-size: 34px; block-size: 34px; border-radius: 50%; 
background: rgba(233, 196, 106, 0.16); }
.ac__icon::before, .ac__icon::after { content: ''; position: absolute; inset: 50% auto auto 50%; inline-
size: 14px; block-size: 2px; margin: -1px 0 0 -7px; border-radius: 2px; background: #E9C46A; 
transition: rotate 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1); }
.ac__icon::after { rotate: 90deg; }
.ac__item[open]:not(.is-closing) .ac__icon::after { rotate: 0deg; }
.ac__a { overflow: clip; }
.ac__a p { max-inline-size: 60ch; padding: 0 24px 24px; color: #CBD3C7; font-size: 17px; line-height: 1.7; 
}

element.js
// E1-58 · Smooth accordion — the markup is plain <details name="faq">, which already works without JS
// (one open at a time, find-in-page opens answers). JS adds smooth height animation: it takes over the
// summary click, removes `name` while active (otherwise the browser snaps the others shut instantly)
// and closes the previously open answer with its own animation.
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
export function init(root) {
  const items = [...root.querySelectorAll('details')];
  const names = items.map((d) => d.getAttribute('name'));
  items.forEach((d) => d.removeAttribute('name'));
  const reduce = () => matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches;
  const body = (d) => d.querySelector('.ac__a');
  function close(d) {
    const b = body(d);
    if (reduce()) { d.open = false; return; }
    d.classList.add('is-closing');
    gsap.to(b, {
      height: 0, duration: 0.38, ease: 'power3.inOut', overwrite: true,
      onComplete: () => { d.open = false; d.classList.remove('is-closing'); gsap.set(b, { clearProps: 
'height' }); },
    });
  }
  function open(d) {
    items.forEach((o) => { if (o !== d && o.open && !o.classList.contains('is-closing')) close(o); });
    const b = body(d);
    const from = d.open ? b.offsetHeight : 0; // re-opening while it was closing: continue from where it  
is
    d.classList.remove('is-closing');
    d.open = true;
    if (reduce()) return;
    gsap.fromTo(b, { height: from }, { height: 'auto', duration: 0.5, ease: 'power3.out', overwrite: true, 
clearProps: 'height' });
    gsap.fromTo(b.children, { y: 10, autoAlpha: 0 }, { y: 0, autoAlpha: 1, duration: 0.5, delay: 0.08, 
ease: 'power3.out', clearProps: 'all' });
  }
  const onClick = (e) => {
    const s = e.target.closest('summary');
    if (!s || !root.contains(s)) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const d = s.parentElement;
    d.open && !d.classList.contains('is-closing') ? close(d) : open(d);
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', onClick);
  return () => {
    root.removeEventListener('click', onClick);
    items.forEach((d, i) => {
      const b = body(d);
      gsap.killTweensOf([b, ...b.children]);
      gsap.set([b, ...b.children], { clearProps: 'all' });
      if (d.classList.contains('is-closing')) d.open = false;

      if (names[i]) d.setAttribute('name', names[i]);
    });
  };
}

element.css
/* E1-68 · Button pack: text roll, entry-point fill, arrow swap, and a stateful submit button. All buttons  
squish on press. */
.bp {
  --bg: #4338CA; --fg: #fff; --accent: #F59E0B;
  position: relative; isolation: isolate; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; overflow: 
hidden;
  padding: 14px 24px; border: 0; border-radius: 999px; background: var(--bg); color: var(--fg);
  font: 700 17px/1.2 var(--font-sans, system-ui); text-decoration: none; cursor: pointer;
  transition: scale 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1), box-shadow 0.3s;
}
.bp:active { scale: 0.96; }
.bp:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 3px; }
/* 1 · text roll — two copies of the label; on hover both move up one line */
.bp--roll { flex-direction: column; gap: 0; block-size: calc(1.2em + 28px); }
.bp--roll .bp__t { display: block; transition: translate 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1); }
.bp--roll .bp__t + .bp__t { position: absolute; top: 100%; color: #FDE68A; }
.bp--roll:hover .bp__t, .bp--roll:focus-visible .bp__t { translate: 0 calc(-1.2em - 14px); }
/* 2 · fill from the entry point (--x/--y set by JS; falls back to the centre) */
.bp--fill { --bg: transparent; --fg: #111827; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 2px #111827; transition: color 0.4s, 
scale 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1); }
.bp--fill::before {
  content: ''; position: absolute; z-index: -1; left: var(--x, 50%); top: var(--y, 50%); inline-size: 
300%; aspect-ratio: 1;
  border-radius: 50%; background: #111827; translate: -50% -50%; scale: 0; transition: scale 0.55s cubic-
bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
}
.bp--fill:hover, .bp--fill:focus-visible { color: #fff; }
.bp--fill:hover::before, .bp--fill:focus-visible::before { scale: 1; }
/* 3 · arrow swap — the arrow leaves in the reading direction and a twin enters from behind */
.bp--arrow { --bg: #111827; }
.bp__arrow { position: relative; display: inline-block; overflow: hidden; inline-size: 1.1em; block-size: 
1.2em; }
.bp__arrow i { position: absolute; inset: 0; font-style: normal; transition: translate 0.45s cubic-
bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1); }
.bp__arrow i + i { translate: 110% 0; }
.bp--arrow:hover .bp__arrow i:first-child, .bp--arrow:focus-visible .bp__arrow i:first-child { translate: 
-110% 0; }
.bp--arrow:hover .bp__arrow i + i, .bp--arrow:focus-visible .bp__arrow i + i { translate: 0 0; }
.bp--arrow:dir(ltr) .bp__arrow { scale: -1 1; } /* same markup, arrow points right in English */
/* 4 · stateful button — label, spinner or check; width is animated by JS */
.bp--state { --bg: #0F766E; justify-content: center; white-space: nowrap; }
.bp--state[aria-busy='true'] { cursor: progress; }
.bp--state.is-success { --bg: #15803D; }
.bp--state.is-error { --bg: #B91C1C; }

// E1-68 · Button pack — most of the pack is CSS. JS does two things:
// (1) .bp--fill: stores where the pointer entered/left (--x/--y) so the fill grows from there and drains  
back there;
// (2) [data-state-button]: idle →  loading (spinner) →  success (drawn check) / error, animating the  
button's width
//     between states so it never jumps, with aria-busy and a polite status message.
// Use `opts.onAction(button)` →  Promise to run the real work (fetch, add to cart…).
import { gsap, MQ } from '@/motion-core/motion.js';
import { DrawSVGPlugin } from 'gsap/DrawSVGPlugin';
gsap.registerPlugin(DrawSVGPlugin);
const CHECK = '<svg class="bp__check" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19  
7.5"/></svg>';
export function init(root, { onAction = () => Promise.resolve(), resetAfter = 2.4 } = {}) {
  const offs = [];
  const reduce = () => matchMedia(MQ.reduce).matches;
  root.querySelectorAll('.bp--fill').forEach((b) => {
    const at = (e) => {
      const r = b.getBoundingClientRect();
      b.style.setProperty('--x', `${e.clientX - r.left}px`);
      b.style.setProperty('--y', `${e.clientY - r.top}px`);
    };
    b.addEventListener('pointerenter', at);
    b.addEventListener('pointerleave', at);
    offs.push(() => { b.removeEventListener('pointerenter', at); b.removeEventListener('pointerleave', 
at); });
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('[data-state-button]').forEach((b) => {
    const label = b.querySelector('.bp__t');
    const idle = label.innerHTML;
    const status = b.parentElement.querySelector('.bp__status');
    let busy = false;
    // swap content and tween the width from the old size to the new one
    const show = (html, cls) => {
      const w0 = b.offsetWidth;
      b.classList.remove('is-success', 'is-error');
      if (cls) b.classList.add(cls);
      label.innerHTML = html;
      if (reduce()) return;
      const w1 = b.offsetWidth;
      gsap.fromTo(b, { width: w0 }, { width: w1, duration: 0.45, ease: 'power3.inOut', clearProps: 'width' 
});
      gsap.fromTo(label, { autoAlpha: 0, y: 6 }, { autoAlpha: 1, y: 0, duration: 0.3, delay: 0.12, 
clearProps: 'all' });
    };


## Page 244

חיים  כפתורים  חבילת· E1-68תלת־ממד  בלי:  1 טייר —  10 פרק
244
    const run = async () => {
      if (busy) return;
      busy = true;
      b.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
      show(`<span class="bp__spin" aria-hidden="true"></span> ${b.dataset.loading || ''}`);
      if (status) status.textContent = b.dataset.loading || '';
      let ok = true;
      try { await onAction(b); } catch { ok = false; }
      b.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
      const text = ok ? b.dataset.success : b.dataset.error;
      show(ok ? `${CHECK}${text}` : text, ok ? 'is-success' : 'is-error');
      if (status) status.textContent = text;
      if (ok && !reduce()) gsap.fromTo(b.querySelector('.bp__check path' ), { drawSVG: '0%' }, { drawSVG: 
'100%', duration: 0.5, delay: 0.15, ease: 'power2.out' });
      gsap.delayedCall(resetAfter, () => { show(idle); if (status) status.textContent = ''; busy = false; 
});
    };
    b.addEventListener('click', run);
    offs.push(() => { b.removeEventListener('click', run); label.innerHTML = idle; b.classList.remove('is-
success', 'is-error'); b.removeAttribute('aria-busy'); });
  });
  return () => offs.forEach((f) => f());
}

element.css
/* E1-69 · Form feedback: floating labels, drawn checks for valid fields, gentle errors, counter, personal  
success panel. */
.fm { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 0.8fr) minmax(0, 1.2fr); gap: 32px 56px; align-
items: start; padding: 10vh var(--pad, 48px); background: #F1F5F9; color: #0F172A; }
.fm__title { font: 700 clamp(32px, 3.8vw, 58px)/1.04 var(--font-sans, system-ui); letter-spacing: -0.02em; 
}
.fm__intro p { margin-top: 12px; color: #475569; font-size: 18px; }
.fm__form[hidden], .fm__done[hidden] { display: none; } /* a class with display beats the hidden attribute  
*/
.fm__form, .fm__done { display: grid; gap: 14px; padding: 30px; border-radius: 24px; background: #fff; 
box-shadow: 0 30px 60px -40px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.35); }
.fm__field { position: relative; }
.fm__field input, .fm__field textarea {
  inline-size: 100%; padding: 26px 16px 10px; border: 1.5px solid #CBD5E1; border-radius: 14px; 
background: #fff;
  color: inherit; font: 500 17px/1.4 var(--font-sans, system-ui); transition: border-color 0.25s, box-
shadow 0.25s;
}
.fm__field textarea { resize: vertical; min-block-size: 110px; }
.fm__field input:focus, .fm__field textarea:focus { outline: none; border-color: #2563EB; box-shadow: 0 0 
0 4px rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.15); }
.fm__field label {

  position: absolute; inset-inline-start: 17px; top: 17px; color: #64748B; pointer-events: none;
